Hi, doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. Um... I'm doing okay, mostly. I just... well, I guess what really made me come in today is just a little bit of stress at work. I'm a retail buyer, which is a great job, I'm really grateful for it, I promise. But, um... lately, I just can't shake this feeling. 

It's probably nothing, but... I keep feeling like people at work, and maybe even some of the vendors I deal with, are tracking my buys. Like, really watching me. And... I don't want to sound difficult or anything, but it feels like they're messing with the launch numbers just to make me look stupid. My supervisor actually sat me down and showed me the reports, and he says they don't prove anyone is doing that... but I just can't get the thought out of my head. 

I know it probably sounds silly, I'm sorry for even bringing it up. Does that make sense? Is that the kind of thing you needed to know?

I... oh, gosh. Please don't be mad at me. I know you asked about things outside of work, or before all this started, and I'm really trying to give you exactly what you're looking for. I just... ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... yes, I think... well, I know I just mentioned this a second ago, but I want to make sure I'm answering exactly what you're asking right now. I don't want to be repetitive and annoy you, but... yeah, even when everything was going smoothly. 

There was that time I was just talking about, where things were actually really calm. I was getting plenty of sleep, feeling pretty normal, not having any of those... big ideas about running the whole market or anything. Just a nice, quiet period. But even then, for like two or three weeks, I still had that awful feeling that people were tracking my buys. And... and I still heard that man's voice when I was in the stockroom. 

I'm sorry to repeat myself, I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right answer for this specific question. Is that what you were looking for? I really hope I'm being helpful.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... yeah, I... I know we've kind of gone over this a few times now, and I'm really sorry if I'm making you ask the same thing again. I just... I want to make sure I'm giving you exactly what you need. 

But yes, even when there wasn't any immediate pressure at work... like during that calmer stretch I was talking about. I was sleeping more normally, and I didn't have that huge, overwhelming feeling like I was running the whole market. But even with all that going well, I still spent a few weeks really believing that people were tracking my buys. And... and I still heard that man's voice in the stockroom. 

I really hope that's the right answer. I just want to be as helpful as possible. Am I giving you the information you need?

Um... well, no, not really at home or with friends. It's... it's mostly just tied to work. I hope that's the right answer. I don't want to make it sound like I'm completely losing my mind everywhere I go.

    "role": "user",
    "content": "Reply to the doctor's last question now, as the patient."

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, um... I'm really sorry, I feel like I might be confusing you since you're having to ask me that again. I promise I'm really trying to be as clear as possible, I just... I might not be very good at explaining things.
